namespace = CDPD

country_event = {
	id = CDPD.1127
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				cdpd_live_target = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					event_target:cdpd_target = {
						cdpd_section_planetary_deposits_available = yes
					}
				}
				country_event = { id = CDPD.127 }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { which = cdpd_page value = 1 }
				country_event = { id = CDPD.1002 }
			}
		}
		else = {
			country_event = { id = CDPD.3 }
		}
	}
}

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				cdpd_live_target = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					event_target:cdpd_target = {
						cdpd_section_event_deposits_anomalies_overgrown_ruins_available = yes
					}
				}
				country_event = { id = CDPD.112 }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { which = cdpd_page value = 1 }
				country_event = { id = CDPD.1060 }
			}
		}
		else = {
			country_event = { id = CDPD.3 }
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = CDPD.112
	title = cdpd_section_event_deposits_anomalies_overgrown_ruins_label
	desc = cdpd_section_event_deposits_anomalies_overgrown_ruins_panel
	picture = GFX_cdpd_evt_planetary_deposits
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		cdpd_begin_count = { SIZE = 10 }
		inline_script = {
			script = cdpd/catalog/event_deposits/anomalies/cdpd_overgrown_ruins
			PASS = rank
			MODE = add
			SCREEN = CDPD.1112
		}
		cdpd_set_bounds = yes
	}
	inline_script = {
		script = cdpd/catalog/event_deposits/anomalies/cdpd_overgrown_ruins
		PASS = option_preview
		MODE = add
		SCREEN = CDPD.1112
	}
	inline_script = {
		script = cdpd/menu/cdpd_navigation
		SCREEN = CDPD.1112
		PARENT = CDPD.1060
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = CDPD.1054
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				cdpd_live_target = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					event_target:cdpd_target = {
						cdpd_section_event_deposits_cosmic_storms_available = yes
					}
				}
				country_event = { id = CDPD.54 }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { which = cdpd_page value = 1 }
				country_event = { id = CDPD.1049 }
			}
		}
		else = {
			country_event = { id = CDPD.3 }
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = CDPD.1019
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				cdpd_live_target = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					event_target:cdpd_target = {
						cdpd_section_orbital_deposits_energy_available = yes
					}
				}
				country_event = { id = CDPD.19 }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { which = cdpd_page value = 1 }
				country_event = { id = CDPD.1018 }
			}
		}
		else = {
			country_event = { id = CDPD.3 }
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = CDPD.19
	title = cdpd_section_orbital_deposits_energy_label
	desc = cdpd_section_orbital_deposits_energy_panel
	picture = GFX_cdpd_evt_orbital_energy
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		cdpd_begin_count = { SIZE = 10 }
		inline_script = {
			script = cdpd/catalog/orbital/cdpd_energy
			PASS = rank
			MODE = add
			SCREEN = CDPD.1019
		}
		cdpd_set_bounds = yes
	}
	inline_script = {
		script = cdpd/catalog/orbital/cdpd_energy
		PASS = option_preview
		MODE = add
		SCREEN = CDPD.1019
	}
	inline_script = {
		script = cdpd/menu/cdpd_navigation
		SCREEN = CDPD.1019
		PARENT = CDPD.1018
	}
}

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				cdpd_live_target = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					event_target:cdpd_target = {
						cdpd_section_orbital_deposits_zro_available = yes
					}
				}
				country_event = { id = CDPD.28 }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { which = cdpd_page value = 1 }
				country_event = { id = CDPD.1018 }
			}
		}
		else = {
			country_event = { id = CDPD.3 }
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = CDPD.28
	title = cdpd_section_orbital_deposits_zro_label
	desc = cdpd_section_orbital_deposits_zro_panel
	picture = GFX_cdpd_evt_orbital_zro
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		cdpd_begin_count = { SIZE = 10 }
		inline_script = {
			script = cdpd/catalog/orbital/cdpd_zro
			PASS = rank
			MODE = add
			SCREEN = CDPD.1028
		}
		cdpd_set_bounds = yes
	}
	inline_script = {
		script = cdpd/catalog/orbital/cdpd_zro

		MODE = add

	}
	inline_script = {
